<!-- specs/1791384155-a-round-record-cell-has-one-reader -->
### 1791384155-a-round-record-cell-has-one-reader

| Corrected · N9 · On a recorded seal the `rounds` row reads `<R> · capped` where `chain_check.says_reopened` answers True for the last record's `Needs a fix` read through `reader.visible`, as the gate reads it (round 1's 🟡 3), `<R>` over the deferral row where it answers False, and `<R>` alone where it answers None — `yes` alone, a word outside the vocabulary, an empty cell — as for a record with no `Needs a fix` row, no readable `## Verdicts`, a `broad-gate.md` home or no record at all; ### 1791384156-config-rows-coordinates-and-headings-have-one-reader

| K1 · `hooks/config.py#config_text` is the one place `seal/config.md` is opened, and it tells two states apart: no file at that name is `(None, None)`, nothing declared; a file that is there and will not read as UTF-8 text — a directory of that name, bytes that do not decode, a symbolic link to nothing — is `(None, sentence)`, the sentence opening with the path. `config_value` (over text) and `value_of` (over rows already walked) answer one item: no row is None, one row is its value as `config_rows` returns it (`""` where empty), and a row written more than once has no value and the refusal "`<item>` appears N times — one value", the sentence a doubled `Pact notify` had; the count is over the rows `config_rows` takes, so an example in a fence above the table is not a second row. `declared_value` is the two together | `hooks/config.py#config_text@974269b9`, `hooks/config.py#unreadable_config@c172fa66`, `hooks/config.py#doubled_row@2a8a6b7d`, `hooks/config.py#config_value@a3dd35a3`, `hooks/config.py#value_of@e9c6790c`, `hooks/config.py#declared_value@7a59cb6e`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_a_mode_row_written_twice_has_no_value@ffc8025d`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_an_absent_config_and_an_unreadable_one_are_two_states@14453dcc`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_one_row_is_its_value_and_no_row_is_none@b76eb209`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_a_fenced_example_of_a_row_does_not_double_the_live_one@4ad64389` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: the four cases red against 5623d728's `hooks/config.py` (`AttributeError` on the new names, and `('mode', 'shared')` for a doubled `Mode`); green after. `mutation-check`, each red: the `len(values) > 1` arm removed, the `lexists` arm removed, the unreadable arm answering `(None, None)` | 2026-10-08 | #867 phase 1. The unreadable fixtures are a directory and a non-UTF-8 file, which hold on macOS, Linux and Windows alike; a permission bit is left out because Windows and a root user read through it |
| K2 · `hooks/config.py#declared_mode` answers `refused`, with the reader's sentence, for a `config.md` that will not read and for a `Mode` row written twice, and `hooks/mode-gate.py#undeclared` reads `refused` as silence, as it read an unreadable file: the gate opens the file nowhere else, `mode-gate.py#unreadable` is gone, and a doubled `Mode` row neither denies nor asks. Prompt budget of the change: zero | `hooks/config.py#declared_mode@99ee670e`, `hooks/mode-gate.py#undeclared@0f5e1f6b`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_a_mode_row_written_twice_is_silence_not_a_question@c5d3b914`, `tests/test_the_mode_question_is_asked_once.py#test_a_config_nobody_can_open_is_silence_not_a_deny@0b88f17c` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: the doubled-row gate case red against 5623d728 (the first row, `whatever`, named no mode and the gate denied), green after; `mutation-check`, each red: `declared_mode`'s refused arm removed, the gate's `"refused"` taken out of its silent pair | 2026-10-08 | #867 phase 1; spec S1 (the hook half) and S3 (the hook half) |

| K4 · `hooks/config.py#pact_declaration` asks `value_of` for `Pact` and `Pact notify`: a `Pact` row written twice is refused in the generic sentence, "`Pact` appears 2 times — one value", and lists no pact, so its entries are neither read nor refused; `declared_pacts` reads through `config_text`, None for a file that will not read and no row for no file | `hooks/config.py#pact_declaration@a040bada`, `hooks/config.py#declared_pacts@16338e3d`, `tests/test_a_signer_declares_its_pact.py#test_a_row_that_will_not_parse_is_refused_and_never_read_as_absent@de57f4d5`, `tests/test_a_signer_declares_its_pact.py#test_a_refusal_comes_after_a_doubled_pact_and_before_the_entries@3e6fab60`, `tests/test_a_signer_declares_its_pact.py#test_an_unreadable_config_is_no_declaration@3017b45a` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: both doubled-`Pact` cases red against 5623d728 (the old sentence, and an entry refusal from the first row), green after; `mutation-check`, each red: the doubled-`Pact` refusal dropped, `declared_pacts`' unreadable arm dropped | 2026-10-08 | #867 phase 1. `docs/the-pact.md` §*How a signer names the pact* already says a row written twice has no value at all |

| Corrected · S7–S10 · a root whose `config.md` carries no `Mode` row is named by a gate: one deny, then one `ask`, then silence for the rest of the session — two prompts per session per repository — and nothing at all once the row exists. | Corrected · C1 · `evidence-check --reverify`, in place and under `--into`, plans every ledger write without making one, records the pact changes the plan owes, and writes the plan only after; it appends one row per re-read ledger row citing a clause of a pact the signer's `Pact` row declares, and per such row with a BROKEN coordinate, to `seal/pact-changes/<work-item-id>.md` (created with its header), and prints a `recorded` line; `Pact notify` filters (`always` adds `—` rows, `never` records nothing); the id is `--into`'s fragment, else the branch's declared work item, else a `LEFT` line and exit 1; a coordinate whose last recorded row for the same clause and ledger row says the same move or the same BROKEN is not appended again, so a change re-landed after its revert is; a record that will not read, decode or parse is named and left; a `seal/config.md` that is there and will not read is refused at exit 2 before anything is planned, by the plugin and by a vendored copy alike, and nothing is re-stamped or recorded; a vendored copy names each citing row, and each other moved row where `seal/config.md` holds a plain `Pact` row and a plain `Pact notify` row that both carry a value, or holds a line that names a pact by `PACT_WORD` (as written or `NFKC(html.unescape)`, the plugin's word and predicate, held equal) and is neither a plain `\| Pact \| … \|` nor a plain `\| Pact notify \| … \|` row, reading a two-cell row (`CONFIG_ROW_RE`, on a GFM line no `str.splitlines`-only character cuts) by its item alone unless it stands directly above a line GFM may read as a delimiter row (`UNDER_A_HEADER`: block-quote markers, a vertical tab or form feed, and a one-column row with no pipe included), where it is a table's header and is read whole with no `\|` asked, and asking no `\|` of a line in a file holding an HTML table cell (`HTML_CELL`), and records nothing; a `Pact notify` row written twice has no value, so `always` cannot be ruled out; a row left whole moved nothing and records nothing; the ledger bytes are what they are without a `Pact` row | `seal/releases/0.19.0.md#"### 1791239490-a-repository-that-keeps-a-pact-is-a-signer">"Corrected · C1 <!-- specs/1791384158-the-broad-gate-reads-its-counts-from-the-recorder -->
### 1791384158-the-broad-gate-reads-its-counts-from-the-recorder

| K1 · the recorder writes on every `test` line a `category`: the first element of what `pytest_report_teststatus` answers for that report, asked with the report and the config exactly as pytest's terminal reporter asks it before counting the report on its summary line — `passed`, `failed`, `skipped`, `xfailed`, `xpassed`, `error` for a failed setup or teardown, `""` for a report pytest counts nowhere, a plugin's own word as given — `""` without asking the hook for a report whose `count_towards_summary` is false, which pytest's terminal reporter leaves off its line (#869 round 1's ⬜ 5), and `None` where the answer holds no word or the hook raised, so nothing is raised out of the hook | `skills/verify/scripts/pytest_record/specseal_pytest_record.py#Recorder@b8ff57d3`, `tests/test_the_recorder_writes_what_its_process_ran.py#test_each_test_line_carries_the_category_pytests_own_line_counts@cb76d1d3`, `tests/test_the_recorder_writes_what_its_process_ran.py#test_a_teststatus_answer_that_holds_no_word_writes_no_category@c9612210` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: the cases pass plain and under `-n 2`, and the record counted the gate's way equals pytest's printed line on the category tree; red at 5623d728's recorder (no `category`); four mutants of `category_of` (the word read from the letter, the hook asked without the config, each guard removed) were each red through `bin/mutation-check`, and in round 1's fix pass the `count_towards_summary` guard removed; a pytest 9 run with `subtests` and an xfail gave pytest's own line, plain and under `-n 2`, with the guard in place; Q-M1 measured the answers equal to the terminal reporter's `stats` on pytest 6.1, 7.0 and 9.1 and under xdist 3.8 (`phases/phase-1.md`) | 2026-10-08 | #869, `spec.md` Scope 1, R4 |
| K2 · the recorder writes a `collect` line for a skipped collection, outcome `skipped`, beside the failed one it wrote before, each under the collector's own path | `skills/verify/scripts/pytest_record/specseal_pytest_record.py#Recorder@b8ff57d3`, `tests/test_the_recorder_writes_what_its_process_ran.py#test_each_test_line_carries_the_category_pytests_own_line_counts@cb76d1d3` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: the case's module that skips itself is one `collect` line with outcome `skipped`; red at 5623d728's recorder; the `elif report.skipped` arm made `False` was red through `bin/mutation-check` | 2026-10-08 | #869, `spec.md` Scope 1, R5 |

| Corrected · B2 · `hooks/sealer-stamp.py` prints `seal_stamp.fitted`'s message, `admitted`'s drawn blocks — each its label, then its block form — joined by a blank line. `admitted` carries, over `(label, rows)` blocks, as many of the oldest pending blocks as fit `MESSAGE_BUDGET` together with their disc. Sizes are UTF-16 units. The rung with no disc (`stamp(rows, disc=False)`) is only for the oldest block alone, when it does not fit with its disc by itself, and is returned whatever its size; so the first pending file is always drawn. `drawings` draws every file whole with its disc before any claim, then claims only the files whose blocks `admitted` carries; the rest stay pending for the next `Stop`. One real run's stamp is 5,254 units with its label, and under the S no two stamps of any panel the cases draw share one message, so one real run goes out per `Stop` and a second waits for the next. Whether a small pair shares a message is the mark's to say, so the case that needs one over the budget grows its panel until it is | `seal/releases/0.20.0.md#"### 1791270164-the-release-seal-is-drawn-in-curves">"Corrected · B2 ·"@34ce5449`, `skills/verify/scripts/seal_stamp.py#admitted@30cf5ddd`, `skills/verify/scripts/seal_stamp.py#stamp@b537ca7c`, `hooks/sealer-stamp.py#drawings@5300a6cf`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_the_hooks_message_is_under_the_budget_for_one_file@57169897`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_two_files_in_one_turn_are_under_the_budget_together@17977478`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_one_seal_too_large_for_the_disc_is_drawn_alone_without_it@b1931aab`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_a_character_outside_the_bmp_is_counted_as_two@2da90fa0`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_several_files_come_out_one_stop_each_oldest_first@8e81a7d3`, `tests/test_the_stamp_reaches_the_person_it_is_drawn_for.py#test_the_ladder_steps_down_in_order_and_ends_with_no_disc@e9d98a4d` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: the cases pass, and the drawing is byte for byte the 0.90 one (K15); the cited claim's ladder, rung, lift and scales were retired by #853 (K16) | 2026-10-08 | Corrected 2026-10-08 by work item 1791384158-the-broad-gate-reads-its-counts-from-the-recorder |

The height is the taller one's and each is centred on it. A cell inside the circle is its colour on both halves, every other cell of the disc's columns is empty, nothing outside the disc has a background, and no line runs past its own last visible cell. There is no sheet, no edge and no frame, and `Letter.disc` is `(0, 2 · top_disc, 28, 28)`. <!-- specs/1791384159-a-unit-the-extractor-cannot-bound-is-refused -->
### 1791384159-a-unit-the-extractor-cannot-bound-is-refused

| U1 · one table decides which rule bounds a unit, by suffix: `bounding_rule` answers "ast" for `.py`/`.pyi`, "heading" for `.md`, and None for every other suffix and for none; `resolve_unit`, `minor_region`, `file_units` and `content_matches` read it and test no suffix themselves | `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#bounding_rule@b869f5a9`, `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#minor_region@2f964300`, `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#content_matches@3ae47b8a`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_the_four_dispatches_read_one_table@9d0c447d` | **Executed** 2026-10-12: the case red against the merge head 1e05daa3, where `bounding_rule` still answered "brace" and "block", and green after; `bin/mutation-check` answering "ast" for an unnamed suffix red | 2026-10-12 | #870, phase 5 |
| U2 · `resolve_unit` answers `Resolution(places, refused)`, no tuple: a bare symbol in any file but `.py`, `.pyi` and `.md` reads "no bounding rule for `<suffix>`; anchor a quoted line instead:" with the row's own path, a placeholder quoted line and a zero hash, the paragraph rule and `--reverify` named; in a `.md` it reads the heading-path sentence; in a `.py` the running interpreter cannot parse it names that interpreter's version and the `SyntaxError`'s line, with no places and no fall-through to a text rule; `judge` prints the refusal as `BROKEN` before the repo-wide scan, and `--reverify` prints it and writes nothing | `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#resolve_unit@2c567801`, `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#unbounded@a16885e2`, `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#Resolution@253d258e`, `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#judge@79532968`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_bare_symbol_outside_the_two_parsers_is_refused_with_the_shape@25494584`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_bare_symbol_in_a_suffix_no_rule_names_is_refused_with_the_remedy@17c06cdd`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_python_file_that_will_not_parse_is_refused_naming_the_interpreter@566c4418`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_bare_symbol_in_markdown_is_refused_and_names_the_heading_path@9b2104cd` | **Executed** 2026-10-12: S3's 22 suffixes red against 1e05daa3 and green after; the sentence's shape mutated red; the Python cases kept from 2026-10-08 | 2026-10-12 | #870, phases 1 and 5 |
| U3 · every caller reads the refusal from `resolve_unit`'s one answer and none derives it: `read_citation` prints it as `BROKEN` for a citation by a bare symbol, `.github/scripts/rider_check.py#region_lines` returns it as its reason, and `survivor_check.py`'s `resolves` reads the empty places as not resolving; no caller calls `bounding_rule`, `py_spans` or `unbounded` | `skills/evidence-check/scripts/evidence_check.py#read_citation@4f77a1f8`, `.github/scripts/rider_check.py#region_lines@a6af1658`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_every_caller_reads_the_refusal_from_resolve_unit_and_derives_none@6af3220a`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_citation_of_a_released_row_by_a_bare_symbol_is_refused@5be7ac99`, `tests/test_a_unit_the_extractor_cannot_bound_is_refused.py#test_a_bare_symbol_outside_the_two_parsers_is_refused_with_the_shape@25494584` | **Executed** 2026-10-08: each case red against d611c1a0; the `read_citation` and `region_lines` branches each mutated red. **Executed** 2026-10-12: the rider check returns the same sentence for each of S3's 22 suffixes | 2026-10-12 | #870 |

<!-- specs/1791384160-a-fix-range-is-its-own-commits-across-a-merge -->
### 1791384160-a-fix-range-is-its-own-commits-across-a-merge

| S7, S8, S9, S15 · `chain_check.own_commits(root, a, b)` returns exactly the commits `git log --ancestry-path --no-merges --no-renames --name-status -z --reverse a..b` lists, oldest first, each with its `(status, path)` entries read positionally and verbatim; a start that does not reach its end gives `[]` and a failing git None. | S10 · three readers of a git listing read each path verbatim: `touched` through `own_commits` (`log -z`), `tracked_at` through `ls-tree -r --name-only -z`, and `call_sites` through `git grep -n -z -I`, read by two partitions on NUL, the first `:` and LF; so with `core.quotePath` on, a unit added in `naïve.py` is a new unit, a call inside it is named by its enclosing unit, and a `Location` naming it resolves when the next round counts a fix of a fix. A `:` inside a path no longer splits it, and a binary file holding the call is left out of the search rather than read as the head of the next match | `skills/code-review/scripts/round_record.py#tracked_at@b29babea`, `skills/code-review/scripts/round_record.py#call_sites@621cbeb6`, `skills/code-review/scripts/round_record.py#touched@526c2a17`, `tests/test_the_fixes_close_the_record.py#test_a_path_git_would_quote_is_read_as_the_path_it_is@9b832265`, `tests/test_the_fixes_close_the_record.py#test_a_binary_file_holding_the_call_hides_no_call_site@0be1416a`, `tests/test_the_fixes_close_the_record.py#test_a_colon_in_a_path_does_not_split_it@83027d8c` | **Executed** 2026-10-08 on macOS, git 2.50: the S10 case red at 361b4e4a against each reader in turn — `New units` `none` (the quoted `ls-tree` name matched no path `touched` read), then, with `tracked_at` verbatim, `helper → caller, "na\303\257ve.py", pytest`, then green with `call_sites` on `-z`. By `bin/mutation-check`, each red: `-z` dropped from `ls-tree` (S10), `-z` dropped from `grep` (S10), `-I` dropped (the binary case), the `Location`'s tracked set read back through the quoting split in `landings` (S10's `Fix of a fix`), and the first `:` read as the last (the colon case, which SURVIVED until that case was added). The colon case is skipped on Windows, which allows no `:` in a name | 2026-10-08 | Added by work item 1791384160-a-fix-range-is-its-own-commits-across-a-merge (#860); `questions.md` Q3 is open — the Windows leg has not run S10 |
